The provided excerpt from RM0041 states for the SRAM/NOR-flash chip-select control registers (FSMC_BCR1..4):

- **Address offset: `0xA000 0000 + 8 * (x – 1)`, x = 1...4**

This expression includes the **base address** `0xA0000000` and does **not** directly give a pure register offset. If we convert it to offsets from the FSMC Bank1 base:

- BCR1 offset = `8*(1-1)=0x00`
- BCR2 offset = `8*(2-1)=0x08`
- BCR3 offset = `0x10`
- BCR4 offset = `0x18`

So for **BCR2**, the **address offset is 0x08**, matching the claimed value `0x8`. Therefore the fact is true.
